## October 27, 2026, 8:59 PM Pacific — Persist E12 datasets during execution

### What was done (commands, files, features)

ephemeral runner. It uploaded `job.log` every 15 seconds but saved `/work/out`

to their existing S3 job prefixes. Downloaded all three archives and verified
their hashes; E12b's first archive contained two draw records. Committed Amendment
16 and a bounded snapshot helper, then installed a low-priority systemd timer on
the two still-running E12a/E12c hosts. Both produced subsequent automatic
snapshots; downloads matched their SHA-256 manifests.

`out/` tree contains all four draw records and 32 files. Downloaded that tree,
checked the producer's completion report and draw count, and archived a final
copy with a checksum manifest. Its raw dataset SHA-256 is
`055811b722f441ab681172a605f9a540b174734dbb4f23ff9f03b931d948c81b`.
Bucket lifecycle inspection found only a 30-day `cache/` expiration rule, with no
expiration for the job datasets.

Code, SSM receipts, immutable snapshot references, and readback evidence are in
`research/fall_degree_bounds_20261008/e12/continuation-20261028/S3_PERSISTENCE.md`
and `persistence/`. Local tests covered archive bytes/hashes, upload failure,
checksum mismatch, job allowlist, and deadline behavior. Shell syntax and
whitespace checks passed. No solver source changed.

### Decisions made (and why)

Snapshot live files about once a minute without restarting solvers. Write unique
archives and manifests, publishing the latest pointer only after checksum
verification. Keep the original final `out/` uploads separate. File snapshots
do not checkpoint solver memory or enable mid-closure resume. Preserve prior
attempts, launch receipts, and negative/error observations. No new instance,
draw, seed, gate, scientific reading, or runtime budget was authorized.

### Open threads (unfinished, pick up next time)

automatic solver retries. Their verified snapshots had no completed new draw
yet. Reconcile current S3/EC2 state before further action. E12b's four records
are safely persisted, but independent scientific review is still pending.

  `rejects_over_folded_fri_at_depth_gate` (S2N-2, with the depth-gate-off
  control accepting the false statement at final size 1). The three existing

  constraint positive control, tampered and forged (solved) evaluations,
  unsatisfying witnesses (gate, copy, Z ≡ 0), non-canonical values. Module docs

  can assert the fold check specifically, not just "FRI failed".
- `check_gated` has a `depth_gate` switch used only by the S2N-2 control; it is
  the cheapest way to show the gate is the sole guard (not redundant).

  the clear in place of KZG commitments (no working pairing), documented.

## 04:35 UTC — weighted-Proj whole-curve integrality gate

### What was done

- Created isolated worktree `/home/ec2-user/crypto-proj-integral-20261028`, branch `codex/proj-integral-20261028`, from `bfb95ba73b90025ee0d16b0a3692a47b84e2ea5e`. Used explicit scoped Conductor task T-681. No PR branch, frozen data, or external research job was touched.
- Added `WeightedProjIntegral.infinity_chart_z_ne_zero`, proving the infinity-chart coordinate `zbar` is nonzero for arbitrary field coefficients. Pinned Lean 4.34.1 exited 0. Direct `#print axioms` lists only `propext`, `Classical.choice`, and `Quot.sound`; no `sorry` or new axiom.
- Added `PROJECTIVE_INTEGRAL` PDF/Markdown/TeX, a neutral SVG/CSV dependency packet, a vector figure, and the direct Lean output log. The two-page PDF was rendered and its graph page visually inspected. Source SHA-256: `02f824eabfb687bd83ab99a6baff6c2083378bdc8d8cf6fb22985277efc5610a`; PDF SHA-256: `ebd7d0307f098e0cdf47e46f1e69ed7ba8567cfcedbb69e2e32b16cf1a4d9d82`.

### Decision and exact open gate

- No whole-curve `IsIntegral` instance is claimed. The checked two-open cover and two chart-domain theorems need a checked nonempty intersection in the actual weighted Proj. The new ring-level nonzero lemma supplies the starting point for the infinity-chart principal open, but its point must be transported through `xBasicOpenIsoInfinity` and identified with the Proj Z-open. Then pinned Mathlib's irreducibility/reducedness gluing APIs can apply. Two domains without the overlap hypothesis can form a reducible disconnected union.
- Global principal divisor, Pic0 class transport, and compiled runtime semantics remain open. No DLP experiment or cost measurement was performed.

  can assert the fold check specifically, not just "FRI failed".

  the cheapest way to show the gate is the sole guard (not redundant).

Only those 17 records change, with 0 regressions; End(E) proved goes from 19,876 to 19,893 and bounded goes to 0.
- Updated the method and counts in `docs/curves/TRAITS.md`. Report, graph and PDF are in `research/curve_traits_descent_levels_20261028/`.
### Decisions made (and why)
- Descent instead of more cycle lengths. For d_K = −7, an 11-isogeny cycle of length 2 fits both f = 4 and f = 8, so cycles alone cannot separate levels 2 and 3.

### What was done (commands, files, features)

  `claude/sheriff-reaudit-20261028`, `research/sheriff/SHF-20261028-0f3f64/findings.yaml`).

  `docs/session-logs/`: clear. Scope grew to `src/zk/stark.rs` (see Decisions).
- `src/zk/stark.rs`: `fri_verify` is now `fri_check(..).is_ok()`; `fri_check`
  returns a `FriReject` reason (Shape, FinalValue, FinalLayer, Opening, Domain,
  Fold). Behaviour unchanged.
- `src/zk/stark_v2.rs`: `Reject::Fri(FriReject)`; `check` calls
  `check_gated(.., depth_gate = true)`; prover knobs `fri_zero_tail` and
  `fri_shift_layer1`; tests `rejects_fri_fold_inconsistency` (S2N-1) and
  `rejects_over_folded_fri_at_depth_gate` (S2N-2, with the depth-gate-off
  control accepting the false statement at final size 1). The three existing
  `Err(Reject::Fri)` assertions now name `FriReject::FinalLayer`.
- `src/zk/plonk.rs` (S2N-10): `verify` rejects non-canonical values, checks
  `a(ζ), b(ζ), c(ζ), Z(ζω)` against the sent polynomials, and checks
  `gate + α·perm + α²·L₁·(Z − 1) = t·Z_H` at ζ. The prover's quotient now
  includes the permutation and `Z(1) = 1` terms (before, it was the gate alone,
  and honest proofs verified only for the identity permutation). Tests: copy-
  constraint positive control, tampered and forged (solved) evaluations,
  unsatisfying witnesses (gate, copy, Z ≡ 0), non-canonical values. Module docs
  and README zk row rewritten so they no longer overclaim.

### Decisions made (and why)

- Refactored `fri_verify` into a reason-returning `fri_check` so the S2N-1 test
  can assert the fold check specifically, not just "FRI failed".
- `check_gated` has a `depth_gate` switch used only by the S2N-2 control; it is
  the cheapest way to show the gate is the sole guard (not redundant).
- PLONK: full fix rather than a "reject everything" flag; polynomials stay in
  the clear in place of KZG commitments (no working pairing), documented.

- Added `research/projective_law_followup_20261022/formal/WeightedProjNonbranchZeroOrder.lean`. It constructs a rational point on the actual weighted-Proj curve through the `Z` basic open, identifies its projective stalk with localization of the affine quintic quotient at the evaluation kernel, and proves the pulled-back `y` is a unit when its value is nonzero. It also transports the existing abstract local norm-order lemma into this actual stalk **under explicit domain and DVR instances**.
- Ran `TMPDIR=/dev/shm lake env lean WeightedProjNonbranchZeroOrder.lean` with pinned Lean 4.34.1 and Mathlib `d13f23b723b8a846827a245b89c10fc7d3f11612`; exit code 0. All seven printed axiom audits contain only `propext`, `Classical.choice`, and `Quot.sound`, with no `sorryAx`. The Lean source SHA-256 is `3aca729afb11cdbf3992dc3db727b8d531922ecf381c423bc6bb6b2d6c3d3910`.
### Decisions made (and why)
- Kept domain and DVR as visible hypotheses in the norm-order theorem. This prevents a conditional local statement from being mistaken for a complete nonbranch multiplicity or global `Pic⁰` proof. The proof uses the repository's branch-stalk pattern and Stacks Project Tags 01HD and 00PD.
- No PDF, SVG, or CSV was generated for an unconditional nonbranch order: that theorem has not checked. This log records the checked partial boundary for review.
### Open threads (unfinished, pick up next time)
- Prove the odd-degree affine quintic quotient `K[x,y]/(y²-f(x))` is a domain without a rational simple-root assumption, then show the nonbranch evaluation maximal ideal is generated by `x-a` after localization. These give the missing actual DVR and uniformizer theorem.
- Pull the proof-side common 2+2 numerator and norm factorization into that actual stalk; then discharge the explicit norm-order hypotheses. A global principal-divisor/Pic0 transport and compiled Rust correspondence remain separate gaps.
